# MHCS Core remediated source-coverage index

This index records full source audit and ID lifecycle for the local approved baseline. It does not implement features or establish full MHCS Core product conformance. Mocks, stubs, interfaces, prototypes, and planning artifacts do not satisfy production requirements.

## Repository evidence and provenance

- E0: No application source, Composer/frontend manifests, configuration, migrations, routes, queues, adapters, storage, or executable tests exist in the target repository; conformance documents and approved context are not implementation evidence.
- Draft outputs and seven specifications compared byte-for-byte unchanged with the supplied draft baseline before remediation.
- Source correspondence is unverified because the declared object is unavailable locally; no mismatch was silently accepted.

## Design coverage ledger

The approved HTML design export was audited by design-rule group, screen/surface, state, interaction, responsive behavior, and accessibility-relevant structure. Prototype names, identifiers, clinical samples, simulated DICOM, alert()/window.print(), connection labels, and mock integrations are not production requirements or evidence.
| Requirement ID | Stable design locator | Implementation-relevant item | Exclusion boundary | Primary work package |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DES-001 | style:tokens | Typography, semantic colors, spacing, density, radii, shadows, and compact/comfortable token behavior. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-002 | master-switcher-bar | Persistent MHCS Core Suite header and member/operator portal switcher. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-003 | portal-member app-root | Member shell with topbar, breadcrumbs, role tools, sidebar, active navigation, user context, and scrollable content. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-004 | nav-member/nav-operator | Grouped member and clinic-operational navigation with active state. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-005 | screen-dashboard | Member dashboard hero, points, active visit, quick actions, and recent history. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-006 | queue-active-box | Member active visit without a public ticket number. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-007 | screen-request | New radiography-session form for site, service, date/shift, purpose/upload, cost, balance, and confirmation. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-008 | screen-history | Searchable session history and member-safe report actions. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-009 | screen-wallet | Points packages/top-up and wallet summary. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-010 | screen-op-queue | Private operator queue with policy-safe data and verification actions. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-011 | screen-op-walkin | Assisted walk-in lookup, registration, payment, ticket print, and queue handoff. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-012 | ai-modal | Result image, automated summary, formal report, close, and export actions. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-013 | portal-operator topbar | Operator station selector, workstation/queue/LCD tabs, connectivity, and user context. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-014 | screen-operator-stage-queue | Concurrent Basic, radiography, awaiting-AI, and result-monitor cards. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-015 | screen-operator-lcd | Paired read-only LCD with public destinations, calls, expiry, and stale state. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-016 | left-sidebar | Operator queue statistics, search, filters, rows, selected state, and badges. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-017 | screen-operator-dicom | Read-only DICOM HUD, viewport, overlays, thumbnails, zoom, invert, grid, and reset. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-018 | screen-operator-generator | Protocol/generator controls, AEC, anatomical program, ready, and emergency interlock. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-019 | operator-workstation-footer | Ready status and explicit reject, retake, and complete/send actions. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-020 | completeModal | Explicit confirmation before complete/send submission. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-021 | switchRoute/switchOpModule | Route/module transitions synchronize visible surface and active navigation. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |
| DES-022 | responsive/overflow | Responsive behavior for fixed columns, tables, filters, modals, and narrow viewports; no media-query breakpoints exist. | Active target; prototype data/scripts excluded. | WP-27 |

### Design exclusions and unresolved meanings

- English/X-ray strings in professional/prototype surfaces are not automatically public requirements; UI-language authority controls member/public output (CON-001).
- No explicit media-query breakpoint system exists; fixed columns, tables, filters, modals, and overflow remain verification targets under DES-022.
- Static HTML cannot prove accessibility semantics; those remain verification obligations under DES-* and UIL-373.

## Duplicate, conflict, decision, and dependency mapping

- Architecture owns shared runtime/module rules; modules own module data/workflows. Repeated statements are cross-referenced, not duplicated.
- UI examples, glossary repetitions, checklists, and design sample copy are evidence/fixtures; governing UIL rules own obligations.
- DOC-029..033 are link-only references; OPR-125..128 move to the dependency register.
- CON-001 records the design-copy/UI-language boundary; no silent compromise is made.
